# Wiktionary Maltese - Letter D_v1 (Refined)

Total Entries: 824

## addebita (v-addebita) 🌟 *(REVISED TARGET ENTRY)*
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to debit, to charge
    **MT**: Poġġejt u rreġistrajt ammont ta' spejjeż jew dejn ma' kont uffiċjali (debit).
- **Alternative Forms**: ddebita
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-bank addebita s-somma tal-ħlas mill-kont tiegħi.
    EN: The bank debited the payment amount from my account.

---

## dab (v-dab)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to melt (become liquid, especially through warmth)
    **MT**: Għab, sab u sar likwidu taħt is-sħana.
  - **EN**: to disappear
    **MT**: Għeb u ma baqax jidher.
  - **EN**: to become emaciated
    **MT**: Sar irqiq u dgħajjef minħabba l-mard jew il-ġuħ.
  - **EN**: to show tender feelings
    **MT**: Wera emozzjonijiet sbieħ u sentimenti ta' kura u imħabba.
- **Alternative Forms**: dieb
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-silġ dab malajr taħt ir-raġġi tax-xemx tas-sajf.
    EN: The ice melted quickly under the summer sun rays.

---

## dabbar (v-dabbar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-b-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to ulcerate
    **MT**: Formajt jew kkawżajt ferita u dabra fil-ġilda maż-żmien.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-ferita dabbart minħabba l-nuqqas ta' kura sanitarja.
    EN: The wound ulcerated due to lack of sanitary care.

---

## dabra (n-dabra)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-b-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: ulcer
    **MT**: Ferita jew ulċera maħmuġa fuq il-ġilda.
  - **EN**: sore
    **MT**: Wagħa' jew ksur f'materjal.
  - **EN**: tear, rent, rip
    **MT**: Qasma, tiċrit jew bdil f'drapp.
  - **EN**: debt [figurative]
    **MT**: Debitu jew flus li għandhom jitħallsu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tabib iddilwa d-dabra fuq id-driegħ bil-mediċina.
    EN: The doctor treated the sore on the arm with medicine.

---

## Dabrani (n-dabrani)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Aldebaran (star)
    **MT**: Għassa u l-istilla ewlenija u brillanti fil-kostellazzjoni ta' Taurus (Aldebaran).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Stilla Dabrani kienet tistgħa bil-lejl fil-kostellazzjoni.
    EN: Aldebaran shone at night in the constellation.

---

## dada (n-dada)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: die, dice (polyhedron used in games of chance)
    **MT**: Kubu żgħir bis-sitt naħat bi tikek użat fil-logħob tal-ażżard u tal-karti.
  - **EN**: cube (object more or less in the form of a cube)
    **MT**: Oġġett solidu f'forma ta' kubu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rema d-dada u ġab sitt punti fil-logħba.
    EN: He rolled the die and got six points in the game.

---

## dafar (v-dafar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-f-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to braid
    **MT**: Ingħaqad u rranġa x-xagħar jew korda billi għaqqad tliet partijiet flimkien.
  - **EN**: to plait (hair etc.)
    **MT**: Għamel nisġa minn fergħat jew ħbula.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Oħtu dafret xagħarha f'denb ta' żiemel sabiħ.
    EN: His sister braided her hair into a nice ponytail.

---

## dafra (n-dafra)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-f-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: plait, braid
    **MT**: Strixxa ta' xagħar jew ħabel imqabbad f'nisġa mibruma (braid).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għamlet dafra sabiħa fil-ġenb ta' xagħarha.
    EN: She made a beautiful braid on the side of her hair.

## dagħa (n-dagħa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-għ-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of dagħa
    **MT**: L-att jew il-proċess tat-tdagħha u tad-dagħwa kontra s-sacred u r-reliġjon.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dagħa kien meqjus bħala reat kbir fil-komunità.
    EN: Blasphemy was considered a major offense in the community.

---

## dagħa (v-dagħa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-għ-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to curse, blaspheme
    **MT**: L-att jew il-proċess tat-tdagħha u tad-dagħwa kontra s-sacred u r-reliġjon.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dagħa kien meqjus bħala reat kbir fil-komunità.
    EN: Blasphemy was considered a major offense in the community.

## dagħaj (adj-dagħaj)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-għ-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: blasphemous, foulmouthed, tending to curse
    **MT**: Persuna li tilmenta, tgħajjar u tuża diskors offensiv f'kull mument.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien raġel dagħaj u n-nies kienu jaħarbu minnu.
    EN: He was a foul-mouthed man and people ran away from him.

---

## dagħam (v-dagħam)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-għ-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become gloomy, sad, melancholic
    **MT**: Sirt kiesaħ, dlamu l-emozzjonijiet u sirt imdejjaq fi spirtuk.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dagħam wara li rċieva l-aħbar dwar l-inċident.
    EN: He became gloomy after receiving the news about the accident.

---

## dagħbien (n-dagħbien)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: whirlwind
    **MT**: Riħ qawwi u rotatorju li jiggwida r-ramel u s-sħab f'ċirku.
  - **EN**: abyss, chasm, cleft
    **MT**: Ħofra kbira u profonda fl-art jew fil-muntanja.
- **Alternative Forms**: degħbien
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dagħbien tar-ramel għalaq it-triq fid-deżert.
    EN: The sand whirlwind closed the road in the desert.

---

## dagħdagħ (v-dagħdagħ)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-għ-d-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to overdo, to overload the mind
    **MT**: Għabba bil-kbir il-moħħ jew il-memorja bl-affarijiet.
  - **EN**: to upset someone, to vex, to anger
    **MT**: Ikkawża skumdità u rabja kbira lil persuna.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-diskors tiegħu dagħdagħ l-imħuħ tan-nies fil-laqgħa.
    EN: His speech vexed the minds of the people at the meeting.

---

## dagħdigħ (n-dagħdigħ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-għ-d-għ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of dagħdagħ
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' dagħdagħ, il-proċess ta' konfużjoni u għeja fil-moħħ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dagħdigħ fir-ras waqqaf dwar id-deċiżjonijiet uffiċjali.
    EN: The mental stress stopped official decision making.

---

## dagħmi (adj-dagħmi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deep (of a colour), darkish
    **MT**: Li għandu kulur skur u kiesaħ u spirtu mdejjaq, dalmi.
  - **EN**: sullen
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' sullen.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kellu wiċċ dagħmi kif ra l-problemi r-raħal.
    EN: He had a sullen face as he saw the village problems.

---

## dagħwa (n-dagħwa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-għ-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: swear, curse
    **MT**: Kliem ta' saħta jew reġistru ta' dagħa kontra xi ħadd.
  - **EN**: blasphemy
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' blasphemy kontra dak li huwa sagru.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Qal dagħwa qawwija meta tilef ix-xogħol.
    EN: He uttered a strong curse when he lost the job.

## dahar (n-dahar)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: back
    **MT**: Il-parti ta' wara tal-ġisem tal-bniedem miż-żaqq u mill-ispallejn sal-qadd.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bata minn uġigħ f'dahru wara li tella' t-tagħbija tqila.
    EN: He suffered from back pain after lifting the heavy load.

## daka (v-daka)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-k-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to refer to something
    **MT**: Għamel referenza jew semma xi ħaġa minn qabel.
  - **EN**: to do something undetermined
    **MT**: Għamel azzjoni li mhix definita fi kliem partikolari.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Daka dak il-każ matul id-diskussjoni tagħhom.
    EN: He referred to that case during their discussion.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-k-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: mule
    **MT**: Annimal tal-razzett prodott mill-ħmar u l-debba (mule).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dakar kien qed iġorr it-tagħbija fil-muntanja.
    EN: The mule was carrying the load on the mountain.

## dakinhar (adv-dakinhar)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: that day
    **MT**: F'dak il-jum jew fil-ġurnata partikolari kkonsidrata fil-passat.
  - **EN**: back then, at that time
    **MT**: B'mod li għandu x'jaqsam ma' back then, at that time.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dakinhar kien hemm festa kbira fir-raħal kollu.
    EN: That day there was a big feast in the whole village.

## dakkar (v-dakkar)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-k-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to pollinate (plants)
    **MT**: Iferrex id-dud u l-polline mill-fjura għal oħra biex jagħti l-frott.
  - **EN**: to pollinate (plants) or inseminate (animals) artificially
    **MT**: Inseminja l-annimali artifiċjalment fil-biedja.
  - **EN**: to influence, inspire
    **MT**: Ispira u jagħti enerġija pożittiva lill-oħrajn.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: In-naħal dakkru l-fjuri kollha fil-ġnien matul ir-rebbiegħa.
    EN: Bees pollinated all the flowers in the garden during spring.

## dal-lejl (adv-dal-lejl)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Root**: l-j-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: last night
    **MT**: Il-lejl li għadda jew fil-ħin ta' bieraħ filgħaxija (last night).
- **Alternative Forms**: dallejl
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dal-lejl kien hemm riħ qawwi fil-kosta.
    EN: Last night there was a strong wind on the coast.

## dalam (v-dalam)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-l-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to get dark
    **MT**: Sirt fid-dlam u sparixxa d-dawl tax-xemx mal-lejl f'żona.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dalam il-ħin hekk kif il-post kien il-ħamsa ta' wara nofs waranofsinhar.
    EN: It got dark as the time reached five in the afternoon.

- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: this morning

  - MT: Dalgħodu qomna kmieni biex immorru għax-xogħol.
    EN: This morning we woke up early to go to work.

- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: very frequently

- **Usage Examples**:

    EN: He used to come visit us frequently during the week.

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kumpanija Daniża li stabbiliet uffiċċju f'Malta.

- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:

- **Usage Examples**:

    EN: The storm caused great damage on national roads.

## dannuż (adj-dannuż)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: harmful

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Sustanza dannuża li m'għandhiex tintuża fl-ikel.

## daq (v-daq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to taste (perceive the taste of)
    **MT**: Ħass u għaraf it-togħma tal-ikel jew tax-xorb mal-ilsien.
  - **EN**: to taste, try (eat or drink a bit of in order to check the taste)
    **MT**: Kiekel jew xrob biċċa żgħira biex jittestja t-togħma.
  - **EN**: to experience, to go through [figurative]
    **MT**: Għadda minn esperjenza jew diffikultà fil-ħajja.
- **Alternative Forms**: dieq, doq
- **Usage Examples**:

## daxkien (intj-daxkien)
- **POS**: interjection
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Used to express surprise or curiosity about an unexpected event. Roughly translates to: “Really?”
    **MT**: Esclamazzjoni użata biex tesprimi sorpriża kbira jew mistoqsija 'verament?' fil-Malti.
  - **EN**: “What's the occasion?”
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala “What's the occasion?”.
  - **EN**: “What’s going on here?”
    **MT**: Klassifikat bħala “What’s going on here?”.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għajjat 'daxkien!' meta sema' l-aħbar mhux mistennija.
    EN: He uttered 'really?!' when he heard the unexpected news.

  - **EN**: to help [with għal]
    **MT**: Għen jew ta appoġġ lil xi ħadd.
  - **EN**: to ask for it [with għaliha]
    **MT**: Aċċetta responsabbiltà jew riskju.

## ddejjaq (v-ddejjaq)

    **MT**: Sar ikkonfuż, imdejjaq u bla interess jew f'dwejjaq.

## dderoga (v-dderoga)

## dderra (v-dderra)

## ddestina (v-ddestina)

## ddeterjora (v-ddeterjora)

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddispra wara li ma sabx soluzzjoni għall-problema.
    EN: He despaired after not finding a solution to the problem.

---

## ddisprezza (v-ddisprezza)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to despise, to scorn, to disdain, to slight
    **MT**: Stmajt b'disprezz u b'nuqqas ta' valur lil persuna jew azzjoni (despise).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddisprezza l-gideb f'kull relazzjoni soċjali.
    EN: He despised lying in every social relationship.

---

## ddistra (v-ddistra)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to distract, to draw away
    **MT**: Neħħejt l-attenzjoni u r-riflessjoni ta' xi ħadd minn fuq ix-xogħol (distract).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-istorbju ddistra lill-istudenti matul l-eżami.
    EN: The noise distracted the students during the exam.

---

## ddisċarġja (v-ddisċarġja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to discharge
    **MT**: Ħallejt barra flus, elettriku jew battilja minn apparat (discharge).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-batterija ddisċarġjat wara bosta sigħat użu.
    EN: The battery discharged after several hours of use.

---

## ddittra (v-ddittra)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to be arrogant
    **MT**: Aġixxejt b'kburija u b'arroganti fil-pubbliku.
- **Alternative Forms**: ddottra
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddittra quddiem sħabu biex juri s-saħħa tiegħu.
    EN: He acted arrogantly in front of his friends to show his strength.

---

## ddiverta (v-ddiverta)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to amuse, to divert
    **MT**: Għamilt kummiedja u ddevertejt flimkien, ddeverta.
- **Alternative Forms**: ddeverta
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddiverta lit-tfal bir-rakkonti sbieħ tiegħu.
    EN: He amused the children with his wonderful stories.

---

## ddivrenzja (v-ddivrenzja)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to discriminate
    **MT**: Għamilt u biddilt id-differenzi fil meħtieġ bejn tnejn min-nies (discriminate).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddivrenzjaw bejn l-għażliet skont il-valur tagħhom.
    EN: They discriminated between the options according to their value.

---

## ddiċifra (v-ddiċifra)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to decipher, to decode
    **MT**: Fissirt kodiċi jew skrizzjoni antika, ddeċifra.
- **Alternative Forms**: ddeċifra
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddiċifra r-ritratt fi ftit minuti.
    EN: He deciphered the image in a few minutes.

---

## ddiġerixxa (v-ddiġerixxa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to digest
    **MT**: Proċessajt l-ikel fl-istonku u użajt in-nutrijenti tiegħu (digest).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddiġerixxa l-ikla qabel ma beda jeżerċita.
    EN: He digested the meal before starting to exercise.

---

## ddmanda (v-ddmanda)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to demand
    **MT**: Talabt b'awtorità u bil-forza li tingħata xi ħaġa (demand).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddmanda r-risposta immedjata mill-amministrazzjoni.
    EN: He demanded an immediate answer from the administration.

---

## ddomestika (v-ddomestika)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to domesticate
    **MT**: Drajt u ddaħħalt annimal selvaġġ biex jgħix mad-dar u mar-razzett (domesticate).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddomestikaw il-kelb selvaġġ b'paċenzja kbira.
    EN: They domesticated the wild dog with great patience.

---

## ddottra (v-ddottra)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to be arrogant
    **MT**: Aġixxejt b'kburija u b'arroganti, ddittra.
- **Alternative Forms**: ddittra
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddottra quddiem il-ħaddiema minħabba l-kariga tiegħu.
    EN: He acted arrogantly in front of the workers due to his position.

---

## ddrammatizza (v-ddrammatizza)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to dramatize [figurative]
    **MT**: Biddilt u preżentajt storja f'forma ta' dramm uffiċjali (dramatize).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddrammatizzaw il-ġrajja nazzjonali fuq il-palk.
    EN: They dramatized the national event on stage.

---

## ddubita (v-ddubita)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to doubt
    **MT**: Kelli dubju u nuqqas ta' fiduċja dwar verità (doubt).
- **Alternative Forms**: iddubita
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddubita mill-verità ta' dik l-istqarrija mill-ewwel.
    EN: He doubted the truth of that statement right away.

---

## ddwella (v-ddwella)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to duel
    **MT**: Ġlaijt u ġġieled kontra persuna f'konfront b'armi fuq mistiedna (duel).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddwellaw fis-sebħ skont ir-regoli l-antiki.
    EN: They dueled at dawn according to the ancient rules.

---

## ddwetta (v-ddwetta)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to sing or play a duet
    **MT**: Singjajt jew daqqajt mużika flimkien ma' persuna oħra f'dwett (sing a duet).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ddwettaw flimkien fuq il-palk tat-teatru.
    EN: They sang a duet together on the theatre stage.

---

## debaħ (v-debaħ)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-b-ħ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to slaughter and sacrifice a victim (usually an animal)
    **MT**: Qatel u offra l-annimal f'ritwali jew għall-ikel.
  - **EN**: to slaughter (in general)
    **MT**: Qatel u offra l-annimal f'ritwali jew għall-ikel.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Debaħ il-ħaruf għall-ikla tradizzjonali tar-raħal.
    EN: He slaughtered the lamb for the village traditional meal.

---

## debb (n-debb)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bear [archaic]
    **MT**: Annimal kbir u b'saħħtu bil-fer mill-familja Ursidae fil-bosk (bear).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-debb kien jimxi fir-reġjun tal-muntanji.
    EN: The bear was walking in the mountain region.

---

## debba (n-debba)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: mare (female horse)
    **MT**: Żiemel femminili (mare).
  - **EN**: Shortening of debba tax-xitan: praying mantis
    **MT**: Speċi ta' insett aħdar bil-saqajn twal (praying mantis).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-debba kienet qed tiġri fil-marġ tar-razzett.
    EN: The mare was running in the farm meadow.

---

## debba tax-xitan (n-debba-tax-xitan)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: praying mantis
    **MT**: Insett aħdar żgħir bil-saqajn ta' quddiem mwaħħla (praying mantis).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-debba tax-xitan instabt fuq il-weraq tal-ġnien.
    EN: The praying mantis was found on the garden leaves.

---

## debbel (v-debbel)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-b-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to weaken
    **MT**: Naqqast is-saħħa fiżika ta' persuna jew annimal, dgħajjef / debbula.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-denb debbel it-tifel għal bosta ġranet.
    EN: Fever weakened the child for several days.

---

## debber (v-debber)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-b-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to negotiate
    **MT**: Attira, pjanat u rranġa dwar ix-xogħol jew persunal.
  - **EN**: to recruit workers
    **MT**: Attira, pjanat u rranġa dwar ix-xogħol jew persunal.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Debber il-ħaddiema kollha li kinux meħtieġa.
    EN: He recruited all the required workers.

---

## debboli (adj-debboli)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: weak, feeble
    **MT**: Li m'għandux saħħa fiżika jew valur sod (weak).
  - **EN**: weak, vulnerable
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' weak, vulnerable.
  - **EN**: weak (not having power to convince)
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' weak.
- **Alternative Forms**: debbuli
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien bniedem debboli li ma setax iġorr it-tagħbija.
    EN: He was a weak man who could not carry the load.

---

## debbolizza (n-debbolizza)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: weakness
    **MT**: L-istat u n-nuqqas ta' saħħa jew reżistenza fil-korp (weakness).
- **Alternative Forms**: debbulizza
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-marda ħalliet debbolizza kbira f'riġlejh.
    EN: The illness left great weakness in his legs.

---

## debbuli (adj-debbuli)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: weak, feeble
    **MT**: Nieqes mis-saħħa jew mill-forza, debboli.
  - **EN**: weak, vulnerable
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' weak, vulnerable.
  - **EN**: weak (not having power to convince)
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' weak.
- **Alternative Forms**: debboli
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Argument debbuli li ma stabbilixxa l-ebda konvinzjoni.
    EN: A weak argument that established no conviction.

---

## debbulizza (n-debbulizza)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: weakness
    **MT**: Nuqqas ta' saħħa fiżika jew mentali, debbolizza.
- **Alternative Forms**: debbolizza
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Wera debbulizza matul il-proċess tax-xogħol.
    EN: He showed weakness during the work process.

---

## debbus (n-debbus)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: metal mace
    **MT**: Bastun jew għodda tal-ħadid u tal-injam b meżizzjoni bil-forza.
  - **EN**: strength, power
    **MT**: Bastun jew għodda tal-ħadid u tal-injam b meżizzjoni bil-forza.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Żamm id-debbus f'idu biex jipproteġi l-għalqa tiegħu.
    EN: He held the mace in his hand to protect his field.

## deber (v-deber)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-b-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to negotiate
    **MT**: Innegozzjajt u rranġajt r-riżorsi u d-dmirijiet minnufih, debber.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Deber dwar il-prezzijiet tal-prodotti fil-merkat.
    EN: He negotiated regarding product prices in the market.

---

## debitament (adv-debitament)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: duly
    **MT**: B'mod legalment korrett u skont ir-regolamenti stabbiliti (duly).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dokument kien debitament iffirmat mill-avukat.
    EN: The document was duly signed by the lawyer.

## debitu (n-debitu)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debt
    **MT**: Ammont ta' flus jew spejjeż li jeħtieġu li jitħallsu lura (debt).
- **Alternative Forms**: debtu
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħallas id-debitu kollu qabel tmiem ix-xahar.
    EN: He paid all the debt before the end of the month.

---

## debitur (n-debitur)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debtor
    **MT**: Persuna jew entità legali li għandha l-obbligu li tħallas id-dejn (debtor).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-debitur rċieva l-avviż uffiċjali mill-qorti.
    EN: The debtor received the official notice from the court.

## Debono (n-debono)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a surname from Italian
    **MT**: Kunjom Malti komuni ħafna u tradizzjonali minn oriġini Taljana (Debono).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Is-sur Debono jgħix fir-raħal tal-Mosta.
    EN: Mr Debono lives in the village of Mosta.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine

  - **EN**: debauch
    **MT**: Ħajja u mġiba nieqsa mill-morali u mimlija traskuraġni fil-pubbliku (debauch).

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deboxxaġni tiegħu kienet ikkritikata mir-residenti.

- **Alternative Forms**: debuxxat
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien raġel deboxxjat li tilef il-proprjetà kollha tiegħu.
    EN: He was a debauched man who lost all his property.

## deboxxjat (n-deboxxjat)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debauchee
    **MT**: Persuna li għandha mġiba deboxxjata, deboxxjat.
- **Alternative Forms**: debuxxat
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deboxxjat kien imbiegħed mill-attivitajiet soċjali.
    EN: The debauchee was distanced from social activities.

## Debrincat (n-debrincat)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a surname
    **MT**: Kunjom Malti tradizzjonali (Debrincat).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: It-tabib Debrincat kien il-president tal-għaqda.
    EN: Doctor Debrincat was the president of the association.

## debtu (n-debtu)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debt
    **MT**: Spejjeż u ammont ta' flus li għandhom jitħallsu lura, debitu / dejn.
- **Alternative Forms**: debitu
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħallas id-debtu kollu lill-bank uffiċjali.
    EN: He paid all the debt to the official bank.

- **Gender**: masculine

  - **EN**: debut (a performer's first-time performance to the public)
    **MT**: L-ewwel preżentazzjoni jew dehra pubblika ta' artist fuq il-palk (debut).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-debutt tal-attur kien irċevut b'applaws kbir fil-teatru.
    EN: The actor's debut was received with great applause in the theatre.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: beginner, novice, newbie
    **MT**: Persuna li tinsab fil-bidu ta meħtieġ tal-karriera u fl-ewwel pass tagħha (novice / newbie).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-debuttant wera talent kbir fit-tieni mossa.
    EN: The beginner showed great talent in the second move.

## debuxxat (adj-debuxxat)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debauched
    **MT**: Li tgħix f'traskuraġni morali, deboxxjat.
- **Alternative Forms**: deboxxjat
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Aġir debuxxat li ma jagħmilx unur fir-raħal.
    EN: Debauched behaviour that brings no honour to the village.

## debuxxat (n-debuxxat)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debauchee
    **MT**: Persuna deboxxjata, deboxxjat.
- **Alternative Forms**: deboxxjat
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-debuxxat neħħa r-riżorsi kollha mill-kont.
    EN: The debauchee removed all resources from the account.

## debuxxaġni (n-debuxxaġni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debauch
    **MT**: Imġiba u ħajja traskurata u bla morali, deboxxaġni.
- **Alternative Forms**: deboxxaġni
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-debuxxaġni fil-belt ma kinetx aċċettabbli mir-regoli.
    EN: Debauchery in the city was not acceptable under the rules.

## debħa (n-debħa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-b-ħ
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: an animal or human sacrifice
    **MT**: Ritwali jew offering ta' annimal maqtugħ f'okkażjoni sagra.
  - **EN**: an instance of sacrificial slaughter
    **MT**: Ritwali jew offering ta' annimal maqtugħ f'okkażjoni sagra.
  - **EN**: “sacrifice” as referring to the Eucharist
    **MT**: Ritwali jew offering ta' annimal maqtugħ f'okkażjoni sagra.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għamlu debħa f'servizz reliġjuż storiku.
    EN: They performed a sacrificial slaughter in a historic religious service.

## dedika (n-dedika)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dedication (note prefixed to a work of art)
    **MT**: Stqarrija jew avviż qasir imqiegħed fil-bidu ta' ktieb jew xogħol art b'rispett (dedication).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kitab dedika sbieħ lill-familja tiegħu fl-ewwel paġna.
    EN: He wrote a beautiful dedication to his family on the first page.

---

## dedikant (n-dedikant)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dedicant
    **MT**: Il-persuna li toffri u tikteb id-dedika f'xogħol ta' valur.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dedikant iffirma l-kopja tal-ktieb bl-id tiegħu.
    EN: The dedicant signed the book copy with his hand.

## dedikazzjoni (n-dedikazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dedication
    **MT**: L-impenn sħiħ, id-dixxiplina u l-għotja ta' ħin b'mod lejali lil kompitu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Wera dedikazzjoni kbira lejn xogħlu f'kull pass.
    EN: He showed great dedication towards his work in every step.

---

## deduttiv (adj-deduttiv)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deductive
    **MT**: Li juża l-loġika u d-deduzzjoni minn fatti ġenerali għal dawk speċifiċi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Metodu deduttiv fit-tħejjija ta meħtieġ tar-riċerka.
    EN: A deductive method in preparing the research.

---

## deduzzjoni (n-deduzzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deduction
    **MT**: Il-proċess loġiku fejn tasal għal konklużjoni abbażi tar-regoli jew tnaqqis ta' spejjeż.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deduzzjoni tiegħu kienet eżatta u sodisfattiva.
    EN: His deduction was accurate and satisfying.

## deffes (v-deffes)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-f-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to push, to thrust
    **MT**: Għamilt daħla b'forza u ddaħħalt oġġett f'post magħluq (thrust / cram).
  - **EN**: to cram
    **MT**: Ddeffest ruħek fit-taħdit u n-negozji tal-oħrajn.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Deffes il-karti kollha fil-kaxxa bil-forza.
    EN: He crammed all the papers into the box with force.

## deffus (n-deffus)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-f-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: narrow vase
    **MT**: Vażett jew reċipjent tal-ħġieġ b'għonq irqiq u narrow (vase).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Poġġew il-fjuri fid-deffus fuq il-mejda.
    EN: They placed the flowers in the narrow vase on the table.

---

## deffusa (n-deffusa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-f-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: narrow part of a funnel
    **MT**: Il-parti rqiqa u l-passaġġ tal-imqass jew tal-lemmiża (funnel neck).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deffusa tal-lemmiża kienet teħtieġ tindif.
    EN: The funnel's narrow part needed cleaning.

---

## deffusi (adj-deffusi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-f-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: intrusive, nosy, meddlesome
    **MT**: Persuna li tħobb tinterferixxi fl-affarijiet tal-oħrajn mingħajr ma tkun mistiedna (intrusive).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien raġel deffusi li dejjem jistaqsi dwar ħaddieħor.
    EN: He was a meddlesome man who always asks about others.

---

## definibbli (adj-definibbli)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: definable
    **MT**: Li jista' jiġi spjegat, iddefinit u kkonfermat bil-miktub (definable).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kunċett definibbli b'mod ċar fl-esperjenza.
    EN: A clearly definable concept in experience.

## definit (adj-definit)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: defined
    **MT**: Żgur, magħruf, eżatt u stabbilit b'mod definittiv.
  - **EN**: definite
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' definite.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Pjan definit li rċieva l-approvazzjoni kollha.
    EN: A defined plan that received all approval.

## definittiv (adj-definittiv)

- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: definitive
    **MT**: Kwalità ta' riżultat jew deċiżjoni li hi konklussiva u finali (definitive).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ir-riżultat kien definittiv u ma baqa' l-ebda dubju.
    EN: The result was definitive and no doubt remained.

## definittivament (adv-definittivament)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: definitely
    **MT**: B'mod ċert, konklużiv u mingħajr ebda ammont ta' dubju (definitely).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Definittivament se nospitaw l-avveniment ix-xahar li ġej.
    EN: We will definitely host the event next month.

## definitur (n-definitur)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: assessor
    **MT**: Uffiċjal jew konsulent f'ordni reliġjuża li jassisti fit-tmexxija (assessor).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-definitur rrakkomanda r-riforma fir-regolamenti.
    EN: The assessor recommended the reform in the regulations.

## definizzjoni (n-definizzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: definition
    **MT**: Spjegazzjoni u tifsira eżatta ta' kelma jew ta' kunċett fil-dizzjunarju.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-definizzjoni tal-kelma kienet ċara ħafna u dettaljata.
    EN: The definition of the word was very clear and detailed.

---

## deflorazzjoni (n-deflorazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: defloration
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' ddeflora f'sens tradizzjonali u mediku (defloration).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deflorazzjoni instabt aċċennata fit-testi antiki.
    EN: Defloration was found mentioned in ancient texts.

---

## deformazzjoni (n-deformazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deformity, deformation
    **MT**: Il-bidla u t-telf mill-forma pulita u naturali minħabba forza jew marda.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deformazzjoni tal-istruttura stabbiliet periklu.
    EN: The deformation of the structure established danger.

---

## deformi (adj-deformi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deformed
    **MT**: Persuna jew oġġett li għandu forma mibdula, miksura u mhux naturali (deformed).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Oġġett deformi li ma kienx jista' jintuża aktar.
    EN: A deformed object that could no longer be used.

---

## deformità (n-deformità)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deformity, deformation
    **MT**: Il-kundizzjoni ta' oġġett jew korp li jinsab f'forma deformata, deformazzjoni.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Bata minn deformità f'riġlejh mill-madwar tat-twelid.
    EN: He suffered from a deformity in his legs from birth.

---

## degradanti (adj-degradanti)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: degrading
    **MT**: Li jnaqqas id-dinjità u l-onor ta' persuna b'mod disprezzjanti (degrading).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienet esperjenza degradanti li ħalliet impatt negattiv.
    EN: It was a degrading experience that left a negative impact.

---

## degradazzjoni (n-degradazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: degradation, demotion
    **MT**: Il-proċess ta' tnaqqis ta' grad u dinjità jew deterjorament tal-ambjent.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-degradazzjoni tal-ambjent hija problema globali.
    EN: The degradation of the environment is a global problem.

---

## degriet (n-degriet)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: decree
    **MT**: Ordni uffiċjali u liġi rreġistrata minn gvern jew awtorità suprema (decree).
- **Alternative Forms**: digriet
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħarġu degriet uffiċjali biex iwaqqfu l-bini fiż-żona.
    EN: They issued an official decree to stop construction in the area.

---

## degħbien (n-degħbien)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: whirlwind
    **MT**: Riħ qawwi u rotattiv (forma alternattiva ta' dagħbien).
  - **EN**: abyss, chasm, cleft
    **MT**: Riħ qawwi u rotattiv (forma alternattiva ta' dagħbien).
- **Alternative Forms**: dagħbien
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-degħbien tar-ramel kien jidher mill-bogħod.
    EN: The sand whirlwind was visible from a distance.

## deha (v-deha)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-h-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to be engrossed, occupied, busy (with)
    **MT**: Sirt impenjat sħiħ f'xogħol jew proġett b'attenzjoni u għaqal kbir.
- **Alternative Forms**: heda
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Deha fir-riċerka tiegħu sal-aħħar sigħat tal-lejl.
    EN: He was engrossed in his research until late hours of the night.

## dehbi (adj-dehbi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-h-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: gold
    **MT**: Li jkun magħmul mid-deheb jew għandu l-kulur isfar u jilma tad-deheb (golden).
  - **EN**: golden (made of gold and/or of its colour)
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' golden.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Libset ċurkett dehbi fuq subgħajha fit-tieġ.
    EN: She wore a golden ring on her finger at the wedding.

---

## dehbieni (adj-dehbieni)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-h-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: gold
    **MT**: Magħmul mid-deheb jew b'kulur isfar jilma, dehbi.
  - **EN**: golden (made of gold and/or of its colour)
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' golden.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien monument dehbieni fil-pjazza uffiċjali.
    EN: It was a golden monument in the official square.

## deheb (n-deheb)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-h-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: gold
    **MT**: Metall prezzjuż u valenti bil-kulur isfar li jilma b'simbolu Au u numru atomiku 79.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Xtraw ġojjellerija tad-deheb mill-ħanut tradizzjonali.
    EN: They bought gold jewellery from the traditional shop.

## dehen (n-dehen)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-h-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: wisdom, good sense, reason, prudence
    **MT**: Intelliġenza, għaqal, għarfien u prudenza kbira fid-deċiżjonijiet (wisdom).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Attenda r-riċerka b'dehen kbir u għaqal.
    EN: He attended the research with great wisdom and prudence.

## dehen (v-dehen)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-h-n
- **Definitions**:

    **MT**: Delejt u ksijt minn żejt b'riħa tajba jew minn kura reliġjuża (anoint).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dehen il-pazjent biż-żejt imberrek fl-isptar.
    EN: He anointed the patient with holy oil in hospital.

## deher (v-deher)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-h-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to appear
    **MT**: Sar viżibbli, beda jidher jew ta impressjoni partikolari.
  - **EN**: to become visible
    **MT**: Sar viżibbli, beda jidher jew ta impressjoni partikolari.
  - **EN**: to show up
    **MT**: Sar viżibbli, beda jidher jew ta impressjoni partikolari.
  - **EN**: to appear
    **MT**: Sar viżibbli, beda jidher jew ta impressjoni partikolari.
  - **EN**: to give an impression
    **MT**: Sar viżibbli, beda jidher jew ta impressjoni partikolari.
  - **EN**: to seem
    **MT**: Sar viżibbli, beda jidher jew ta impressjoni partikolari.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Deher fil-post fil-ħin miftiehem mat-tim tiegħu.
    EN: He showed up at the location at the agreed time with his team.

## dehes (v-dehes)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-h-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to thrust in
    **MT**: Daħħalt b'forza u b'saħħa oġġett f'post narrow, deffes.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dehes il-kaxxa fit-toqba tal-ħajt b'forza.
    EN: He thrust the box into the wall hole with force.

- **POS**: verb

    **MT**: Stordajt u tajt sorpriża jew biża' temporanja lil persuna (startle).

  - MT: Dehex lit-tifel bil-ħoss qawwi tal-bieb.

- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-h-n

    EN: A very narrow passage where only one person can pass.

- **Root**: d-w-m

- **Usage Examples**:

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dejjud ssorpriża lill-poplu meta sar jaf verament.
    EN: The cuckold surprised the people when he truly found out.

- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-j-l

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Walk fit-tarf u fid-dejl tal-muntanja wara nofsinhar.
    EN: He walked along the skirt of the mountain in the afternoon.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-w-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: standing army

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dejma kienet responsabbli mid-difiża tal-gżejjer.

## dejn (n-dejn)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-j-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debt (money owed
    **MT**: Flus jew obbligu li wieħed irid iħallas lil oħrajn.

    **MT**: Flus jew obbligu li wieħed irid iħallas lil oħrajn.

    **MT**: Flus jew obbligu li wieħed irid iħallas lil oħrajn.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħallas id-dejn kollu li kellu mal-bank prinċipali.

- **POS**: verb

  - MT: Ħallas id-dejn kollu li kellu mal-bank prinċipali.
    EN: He paid all the debt he had with the main bank.

- **Gender**: masculine

    **MT**: Kastell, fortizza jew monasteru antik u sagru.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dejr storiku kien mibni fuq il-quċċata tal-muntanja.

- **Definitions**:

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kultura dekadenti li ma kellhiex l-istess saħħa preċedenti.
    EN: A decadent culture that lacked its previous strength.

## dekadentiżmu (n-dekadentiżmu)

- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: decadence

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dekadentiżmu kien influwenza kbira fuq il-poeti fil-passat.
    EN: Decadence was a major influence on poets in the past.

- **Gender**: feminine

    **MT**: Il-proċess ta' telf, deterjorament u tnaqqis tal-valuri f'soċjetà.
- **Usage Examples**:

## dekoratur (n-dekoratur)

- **Gender**: masculine

- **Alternative Forms**: demb

    EN: The cat began moving its tail when it saw the food.

## deroga (n-deroga)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: derogation
    **MT**: Eċċezzjoni meħuda minn liġi jew regola uffiċjali għal perjodu temporanju (derogation).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ingħatat deroga speċjali għall-użu tal-ħamrija.
    EN: A special derogation was granted for soil use.

---

## derogabbli (adj-derogabbli)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: derogatable
    **MT**: Regola jew artiklu legali li jista' jiġi sospiż b'deroga (derogatable).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Klawżola derogabbli fi ftehim internazzjonali.
    EN: A derogatable clause in an international agreement.

---

## derogatorju (adj-derogatorju)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: derogatory
    **MT**: Kumment jew diskors offensiv li jnaqqas l-onor ta' persuna (derogatory).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienu kummenti derogatorji li nstabu mhux xierqa.
    EN: They were derogatory comments found inappropriate.

---

## derogattiv (adj-derogattiv)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: derogative
    **MT**: Li jnaqqas il-valur jew l-onor f'komunikazzjoni, derogatorju.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Użu ta' lingwaġġ derogattiv fir-risposta tiegħu.
    EN: Use of derogative language in his response.

---

## derogazzjoni (n-derogazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: derogation
    **MT**: L-att u l-proċess uffiċjali ta' deroga mir-regolamenti, deroga.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-derogazzjoni kienet ikkonfermata mir-reġistru uffiċjali.
    EN: The derogation was confirmed by the official register.

---

## derra (v-derra)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-r-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to thresh or winnow (separate the grain from the chaff, regardless of manner)
    **MT**: Draat u firxejt u sseparajt il-qamħ mil-tiben permezz tar-riħ fl-għalqa (thresh / winnow).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Derra l-qamħ fil-qiegħa matul il-jum bir-riħ.
    EN: He threshed the wheat on the threshing floor during the windy day.

---

## derriesa (n-derriesa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-r-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: an implement used for threshing
    **MT**: Għodda tradizzjonali tal-injam u l-ħadid użata fl-għalqa biex tiderra l-qamħ (threshing tool).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Użaw id-derriesa fl-għalqa wara l-ħsad tal-qamħ.
    EN: They used the threshing tool in the field after harvesting wheat.

---

## deru (n-deru)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: lentisk, mastic shrub (Pistacia lentiscus)
    **MT**: Pjanta u arbust b'riħa karatteristika fil-kampanja Maltija (Pistacia lentiscus).
  - **EN**: mastik (the tree's resin)
    **MT**: Pjanta u arbust b'riħa karatteristika fil-kampanja Maltija (Pistacia lentiscus).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deru kien jikber madwar il-blat u l-għelieqi f'Malta.
    EN: Mastic shrub was growing around rocks and fields in Malta.

---

## dervix (n-dervix)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-r-w-x
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dervish
    **MT**: Membru ta' ordni reliġjuża Iżlamika magħrufa għall-ħajja ta' umiltà (dervish).
- **Alternative Forms**: derwix
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dervix kien jagħmel iż-żfin tradizzjonali solenni.
    EN: The dervish was performing the solemn traditional dance.

---

## derwex (v-derwex)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-r-w-x
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make crazy
    **MT**: Għamilt lil xi ħadd jitlef moħħu u jsir crazy minħabba emozzjonijiet jew marda.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Derwex lil kulħadd fl-uffiċċju bir-rekwiżiti tiegħu.
    EN: He drove everyone in the office crazy with his requirements.

---

## derwix (n-derwix)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-r-w-x
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dervish
    **MT**: Membru ta' ordni reliġjuża f'umiltà, dervix.
- **Alternative Forms**: dervix
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-derwix kien qed jitlob fis-silenzju tal-bini.
    EN: The dervish was praying in the silence of the building.

---

## deskrizzjoni (n-deskrizzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: description
    **MT**: Stqarrija jew test bil-miktub li jagħti d-dettalji kollha ta' oġġett jew persuna (description).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għata deskrizzjoni eżatta tal-persuna l-mifdula.
    EN: He gave an exact description of the missing person.

---

## dessies (n-dessies)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: spindle
    **MT**: Għodda tradizzjonali tal-injam użata biex tidwir it-tajjara u n-nisiġ (spindle).
- **Alternative Forms**: dussies
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għamllet u użat id-dessies fin-nisiġ tas-suf.
    EN: She made and used the spindle in weaving wool.

---

## destabbilizzazzjoni (n-destabbilizzazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: destabilisation
    **MT**: Il-proċess fejn tiġi sospiża l-istabbiltà f'pajjiż jew fl-ekonomija (destabilisation).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-destabbilizzazzjoni tal-merkat ġabet telfien finanzjarju.
    EN: The destabilisation of the market brought financial loss.

---

## destin (n-destin)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: destiny, fate
    **MT**: Il-futur u l-kors tal-avvenimenti determinati fil-ħajja ta' persuna (destiny / fate).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Emmen li kien id-destin tiegħu li jiltaqa' magħha.
    EN: He believed it was his destiny to meet her.

---

## destinatarju (n-destinatarju)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: addressee
    **MT**: Il-persuna jew l-uffiċċju li lilu huma mibgħuta l-ittri jew il-pakkett (addressee).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-destinatarju rċieva l-pakkett bil-posta uffiċjali.
    EN: The addressee received the package via official post.

---

## destinazzjoni (n-destinazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: destination
    **MT**: Il-post u t-terminal finali fejn persuna jew vapur ikun qed ivvjaġġa (destination).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Waslu fid-destinazzjoni tagħhom wara vjaġġ twil fl-oċean.
    EN: They arrived at their destination after a long journey at sea.

---

## detall (n-detall)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: detail
    **MT**: Fattur u biċċa informazzjoni żgħira f'pjan jew f'deskrizzjoni, dettall.
- **Alternative Forms**: dettall
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Żied detall wieħed mar-rapport xjentifiku.
    EN: He added one detail to the scientific report.

---

## deterjorament (n-deterjorament)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deterioration
    **MT**: Il-proċess ta' tnaqqis ta' kwalità jew saħħa maż-żmien (deterioration).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deterjorament tal-bini kellu bżonn intervent immedjat.
    EN: The deterioration of the building needed immediate intervention.

---

## determinabbli (adj-determinabbli)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: determinable
    **MT**: Li jista' jiġi stabbilit u kkonfermat bil-miktub (determinable).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ammont determinabbli abbażi tal-fatti u d-data.
    EN: A determinable amount based on facts and data.

---

## determinanti (n-determinanti)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: determiner
    **MT**: Fattur jew regola prinċipali fil-grammatika jew fis-sitwazzjonijiet li tiddetermina r-riżultat.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-determinanti kien il-fattur prinċipali fil-proċess.
    EN: The determiner was the main factor in the process.

---

## determinattiv (adj-determinattiv)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: decisive
    **MT**: Fattur li jagħti l-konklużjoni u d-deċiżjoni finali (decisive / definite).
  - **EN**: definite
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' definite.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Mossa determinattiva li rrebħet il-kompetizzjoni.
    EN: A decisive move that won the competition.

---

## determinazzjoni (n-determinazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: determination
    **MT**: Ir-rieda sod u d-dixxiplina kbira biex tikseb għan u tikkunsidra deċiżjoni.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Wera determinazzjoni kbira biex itemm l-istudji.
    EN: He showed great determination to finish his studies.

---

## determinista (n-determinista)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: determinist
    **MT**: Persuna li temmen fid-determiniżmu bħala filosofija ta' ħajja.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-determinista stabbilixxa l-argumenti tiegħu fit-test.
    EN: The determinist established his arguments in the text.

---

## determiniżmu (n-determiniżmu)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: determinism
    **MT**: Teorija filosofika fejn kull avveniment huwa determinat minn kawżi preċedenti.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-determiniżmu kien diskuss b'reqqa fil-lezzjoni.
    EN: Determinism was discussed thoroughly in the lesson.

---

## detestazzjoni (n-detestazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: detestation
    **MT**: Sentiment sħiħ ta' mibegħda u disprezz kontra azzjoni uffiċjali (detestation).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-attitudni tiegħu stabbiliet detestazzjoni fl-uffiċċju.
    EN: His attitude established detestation in the office.

---

## dettaljatament (adv-dettaljatament)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: in detail
    **MT**: Bid-dettalji kollha u b'reqqa kbira f'rapport (in detail).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Spjega d-dokument dettaljatament matul il-laqgħa.
    EN: He explained the document in detail during the meeting.

---

## dettall (n-dettall)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: detail
    **MT**: Biċċa żgħira u speċifika ta' informazzjoni f'pjan jew deskrizzjoni (detail).
- **Alternative Forms**: detall
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Neħħew kull dettall mhux xieraq mir-rapport.
    EN: They removed every inappropriate detail from the report.

---

## dettatura (n-dettatura)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dictation
    **MT**: L-att jew il-proċess fejn persuna tiddetta l-kliem biex jinkitbu mill-oħrajn (dictation).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-istudenti wettqu d-dettatura fil-klassi tal-Malti.
    EN: Students carried out the dictation in the Maltese class.

---

## devalutazzjoni (n-devalutazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: devaluation
    **MT**: L-att u l-proċess uffiċjali ta' tnaqqis fil-valur tal-munita (devaluation).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-devalutazzjoni tal-munita ġabet żieda fil-prezzijiet.
    EN: Devaluation of the currency brought an increase in prices.

---

## devastatur (n-devastatur)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: destroyer, devastator
    **MT**: Persuna jew armata li ddistruzzjonat u ddevastat ir-reġjun sħiħ.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-devastatur kien mibgħud mill-poplu kollu.
    EN: The devastator was hated by all the people.

---

## devastazzjoni (n-devastazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: devastation
    **MT**: Il-ħsara kbira, d-distruzzjoni u d-devastazzjoni wara maltempata jew gwerra.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-devastazzjoni kienet viżibbli f'kull kantuniera tal-belt.
    EN: Devastation was visible in every corner of the city.

---

## devjazzjoni (n-devjazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deviation
    **MT**: L-att ta' bidla fir-rotta jew fil-passaġġ prinċipali, bidla fir-rotta.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm devjazzjoni fit-traffiku minħabba l-bini tat-triq.
    EN: There was a traffic deviation due to road construction.

---

## dewa (v-dewa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to sound, to ring, to echo
    **MT**: Ħriġt u rroddajt ħoss, eka u reverberazzjoni miftuħa fl-arja (echo / sound).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dewa l-ħoss tal-qanpiena mal-widien kollha.
    EN: The sound of the bell echoed across all the valleys.

---

## dewba (n-dewba)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-w-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: a melting
    **MT**: In-nom verbal u l-att waħdieni ta' inħall ta' solidu f'likwidu (melting).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dewba tas-silġ bdiet hekk kif tlajt ix-xemx.
    EN: The melting of the ice began as the sun rose.

---

## dewbien (n-dewbien)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-w-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of dab
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' dab, il-proċess ta' inħall ta' solidu minħabba s-sħana.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dewbien tal-butir fl-għaġin tah togħma mill-isbaħ.
    EN: The melting of butter in the pasta gave it a wonderful taste.

---

## dewm (n-dewm)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-w-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of dam and diem
    **MT**: L-att tad-dewmien, l-estensjoni tal-ħin u d-durata.
  - **EN**: duration
    **MT**: L-att tad-dewmien, l-estensjoni tal-ħin u d-durata.
  - **EN**: procrastination
    **MT**: L-att tad-dewmien, l-estensjoni tal-ħin u d-durata.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dewm tax-xogħol kien kawża ta' dewmien fil-proġett.
    EN: The delay of the work was the cause of project delays.

---

## dewma (n-dewma)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-w-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: duration
    **MT**: Durata u perjodu ta' ħin meħtieġ (forma alternattiva ta' dawma).
  - **EN**: delay
    **MT**: Durata u perjodu ta' ħin meħtieġ (forma alternattiva ta' dawma).
- **Alternative Forms**: dawma
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dewma tal-vjaġġ kinet ta' żewġ siegħat.
    EN: The duration of the trip was two hours.

---

## dewmien (n-dewmien)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-w-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of dam and diem
    **MT**: Telf ta' ħin u procrastinazzjoni f'eżekuzzjoni ta' impenn.
  - **EN**: duration
    **MT**: Telf ta' ħin u procrastinazzjoni f'eżekuzzjoni ta' impenn.
  - **EN**: procrastination
    **MT**: Telf ta' ħin u procrastinazzjoni f'eżekuzzjoni ta' impenn.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm dewmien kbir fuq il-vjaġġ tal-vapur minħabba r-riħ.
    EN: There was a major delay on the boat trip due to the wind.

---

## dewqan (n-dewqan)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-w-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of daq
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' daq, il-proċess u l-att ta' teħid ta' togħma mill-ikel.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dewqan tal-inbid sar matul il-mawra fil-fabbrika.
    EN: The wine tasting took place during the factory tour.

---

## dewqien (n-dewqien)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-w-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of daq
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' daq, dewqan / tasting.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dewqien tal-ikel kien suċċess kbir fil-fiera.
    EN: The food tasting was a great success at the fair.

---

## dewwa (v-dewwa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to cure, treat, help (someone wounded or their wounds, someone ill or their issues)
    **MT**: Fejjaqt u tajt il-mediċina u l-kura lil persuna morda jew b'ferita (cure / treat).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dewwa l-pazjent b'sengħa kbira u neħħalu l-uġigħ.
    EN: He treated the patient with great skill and removed his pain.

---

## dewwaq (v-dewwaq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make someone taste something
    **MT**: Għamel lil xi ħadd jidq jew jesperjenza sbuħija jew marda.
  - **EN**: to cause someone experience something
    **MT**: Għamel lil xi ħadd jidq jew jesperjenza sbuħija jew marda.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dewwaq lil sħabu mill-istufat tradizzjonali li saqas.
    EN: He made his friends taste the traditional stew he cooked.

---

## dewweb (v-dewweb)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to melt, to dissolve
    **MT**: Biddel u għamel likwidu minn solidu billi saħħnu.
  - **EN**: to squander
    **MT**: Biddel u għamel likwidu minn solidu billi saħħnu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dewweb iċ-ċikkulata fil-borma biex jagħmel il-kejk.
    EN: He melted the chocolate in the pot to make the cake.

---

## dewwed (v-dewwed)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-d
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to be full in worms or lice
    **MT**: Sirt mimli bid-dud wara li prodott ikel baqa' barra għal żmien twil.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-frott dewwed għax ma żammux fil-friġġ.
    EN: The fruit became wormy because they didn't keep it in the fridge.

---

## dewwem (v-dewwem)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to delay
    **MT**: Żamm u zamm lil xi ħadd jistenna f'pożizzjoni f meżizzjoni.
  - **EN**: to detain
    **MT**: Żamm u zamm lil xi ħadd jistenna f'pożizzjoni f meżizzjoni.
  - **EN**: to keep (someone) waiting
    **MT**: Żamm u zamm lil xi ħadd jistenna f'pożizzjoni f meżizzjoni.
- **Alternative Forms**: dawwem
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dewwem id-deċiżjoni uffiċjali sal-ġimgħa li ġejja.
    EN: He delayed the official decision until next week.

---

## dewwiem (adj-dewwiem)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-w-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: late, slow
    **MT**: Li jdum fit-tul u li għandu r-reżistenza u l-kontinwità maż-żmien (lasting / constant).
  - **EN**: causing delay
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' causing delay.
  - **EN**: lasting, constant
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' lasting, constant.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ftehim dewwiem li ġab il-paċi fir-reġjun.
    EN: A lasting agreement that brought peace to the region.

---

## dewwiema (n-dewwiema)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-w-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: weather vane
    **MT**: Għodda dekorattiva tal-metall maħduma f'forma ta' għasfur li tiddawwar mar-riħ (weather vane).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dewwiema fuq il-bejt kienet turik id-direzzjoni tar-riħ.
    EN: The weather vane on the roof showed the wind direction.

---

## dewwiema tar-riħ (n-dewwiema-tar-riħ) 🌟 *(REVISED TARGET ENTRY)*
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: synonym of dewwiema: weather vane
    **MT**: Għodda li tiddawwar mar-riħ fuq il-bejt, dewwiema.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dewwiema tar-riħ bdiet tiddawwar b'forza minħabba r-riħ qawwi.
    EN: The weather vane began spinning forcefully due to the strong wind.

---

## dewwiemi (adj-dewwiemi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-w-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: procrastinatory
    **MT**: Li għandu t-tendenza li jdawwem u jesperjenza ritard kontinwu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Komportament dewwiemi fl-imġiba tiegħu.
    EN: Procrastinatory behaviour in his conduct.

---

## deċentrament (n-deċentrament)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: decentralization
    **MT**: Il-proċess ta' deċentralizzazzjoni tas-servizzi, ddeċentra.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deċentrament tal-gvern saħħaħ il-kunsilli lokali.
    EN: The decentralization of government strengthened local councils.

---

## deċifrabbli (adj-deċifrabbli)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: decipherable
    **MT**: Li jista' jiġi deċifrat u mil-faċli biex tifhimha f'kodiċi (decipherable).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Messaġġ deċifrabbli mill-inġiniera tas-sistema.
    EN: A decipherable message by the system engineers.

## deċiframent (n-deċiframent)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: decipherment
    **MT**: Il-proċess ta' qari u tifsira ta' kodiċi jew skrizzjoni, deċifrazzjoni.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deċiframent tat-test antik ħa xhur ta' riċerka.
    EN: The decipherment of the ancient text took months of research.

---

## deċifratur (n-deċifratur)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: decipherer
    **MT**: Persuna speċjalizzata li taqra u tiddeċifra l-kodiċijiet (decipherer).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deċifratur sab it-tifsira segrieta tat-test.
    EN: The decipherer found the secret meaning of the text.

---

## deċifrazzjoni (n-deċifrazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: decipherment
    **MT**: L-att ta' deċiframent minn kodiċi, deċiframent.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deċifrazzjoni tal-iskrizzjoni kienet ikkonfermata.
    EN: The decipherment of the inscription was confirmed.

## deċiljun (num-deċiljun)
- **POS**: numeral
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: decillion
    **MT**: Numru kbir enormi li huwa ugwali għal 1 segwit minn 33 jew 60 żero fil-matematika.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Numru deċiljun jintuża f'kalkoli astronomiċi kbar.
    EN: A decillion is used in large astronomical calculations.

---

## deċiż (adj-deċiż)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: determined
    **MT**: Persuna sod li għandha determinazzjoni u li ħadet deċiżjoni finali (determined / resolute).
  - **EN**: firm
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' firm.
  - **EN**: resolute
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' resolute.
  - **EN**: decided
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' decided.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien raġel deċiż li ma jbiddel id-deċiżjoni tiegħu għal xejn.
    EN: He was a resolute man who would not change his decision for anything.

---

## deċiżjoni (n-deċiżjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: decision
    **MT**: L-għażla finali u s-sentenza kkonfermata wara riflessjoni u konsultazzjoni (decision).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ħadu deċiżjoni uffiċjali biex jibdew il-proġett l-ġdid.
    EN: They made an official decision to start the new project.

---

## deġenerazzjoni (n-deġenerazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: degeneration
    **MT**: Il-proċess ta' tnaqqis ta' kwalità, saħħa jew morali (degeneration).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deġenerazzjoni tat-tessut kellha bżonn intervent mediku.
    EN: The degeneration of the tissue required medical intervention.

---

## deżert (n-deżert)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: desert
    **MT**: Territorju u spazju kbir niexef, bla ilma u bla siġar.
  - **EN**: uninhabited place [figurative]
    **MT**: Territorju u spazju kbir niexef, bla ilma u bla siġar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deżert tas-Saħara huwa l-akbar post niexef fl-Affrika.
    EN: The Sahara desert is the largest dry place in Africa.

---

## deżolazzjoni (n-deżolazzjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: desolation
    **MT**: Stat u sensazzjoni ta' abbandun sħiħ, tristizza u nuqqas ta' ħajja (desolation).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-deżolazzjoni kienet tinħass fit-toroq abbandunati tal-belt.
    EN: Desolation was felt in the abandoned city streets.

---

## dfin (n-dfin)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-f-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of difen
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' difen, l-att u r-rit ta' tqegħid ta' ġisem mejjet fil-qabar (burial).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dfin sar fil-preżenza tal-familja kollha u sħab.
    EN: The burial took place in the presence of the entire family and friends.

---

## dfir (n-dfir)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-f-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of dafar: plaiting
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' dafar, il-proċess ta' nisġa u formazzjoni ta' dafra (plaiting).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dfir tax-xagħar jeħtieġ l-għaqal u r-reqqa.
    EN: The braiding of hair requires skill and precision.

---

## dgħajjef (adj-dgħajjef)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-għ-f
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: weak
    **MT**: Persuna jew oġġett li m'għandux saħħa fiżika jew valur sod, debboli.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien tifel dgħajjef minħabba l-marda li kellu.
    EN: He was a weak child due to the illness he had.

---

## dgħajjef (v-dgħajjef)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-għ-f
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to weaken
    **MT**: Naqqast u neħħejt is-saħħa u l-forza ta' persuna jew sistema (weaken).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-denb dgħajjef il-korp kollu tal-pazjent.
    EN: The fever weakened the patient's entire body.

---

## dgħajsa (n-dgħajsa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: boat
    **MT**: Vettura u dgħajsa tradizzjonali tal-injam użata biex taqsam il-port u l-baħar (boat).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rkbu d-dgħajsa u qasmu mill-Kunsill lejn il-Valletta.
    EN: They boarded the boat and crossed from the council to Valletta.

---

## dgħam (v-dgħam)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-għ-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become dark, obscure
    **MT**: Sirt fid-dlam, kiesaħ u b'nuqqas ta meħtieġ ta' dawl, dalam.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Is-sema dgħam hekk kif daħal il-lejl.
    EN: The sky became dark as night arrived.

---

## dgħief (v-dgħief)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-għ-f
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become weak
    **MT**: Sirt dgħajjef fis-saħħa u naqas il-piż jew il-forza fil-korp (weaken).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dgħief fil-korp minħabba n-nuqqas ta' ikel xieraq.
    EN: He became weak in body due to lack of proper food.

---

## dgħif (adj-dgħif)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-għ-f
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: lean (of meat: containing little fat)
    **MT**: Laħam irqiq u nieqes mix-xaħam jew persuna b'korp irqiq ħafna (lean).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Xtara laħam dgħif minn għand il-biċċier.
    EN: He bought lean meat from the butcher.

---

## dgħjufija (n-dgħjufija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: weakness
    **MT**: L-istat u n-nuqqas ta' saħħa fiżika jew emozzjonali fil-bniedem (weakness).
- **Alternative Forms**: dgħufija
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien jagħfsulu dgħjufija f'riġlejh wara l-vjaġġ.
    EN: He felt weakness in his legs after the trip.

---

## dgħufija (n-dgħufija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-għ-f
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: weakness
    **MT**: Nuqqas ta' saħħa jew reżistenza, dgħjufija.
- **Alternative Forms**: dgħjufija
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Wera dgħufija fil-karattru matul id-dibattitu.
    EN: He showed weakness in character during the debate.

---

## dgħum (n-dgħum)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-għ-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of dagħam
    **MT**: L-att tad-dlam u tad-dwejjaq tal-moħħ u l-post.
  - **EN**: gloominess
    **MT**: L-att tad-dlam u tad-dwejjaq tal-moħħ u l-post.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dgħum tal-kamra kien iġib is-skiet fis-salott.
    EN: The gloominess of the room was bringing silence in the living room.

## dgħumi (adj-dgħumi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-għ-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sullen, gloomy, depressed
    **MT**: Persuna jew wiċċ b'imġiba mdejjqa, skura u nieqsa mil-ferħ, dagħmi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien raġel dgħumi li ma kienx itkellem fil-laqgħa.
    EN: He was a sullen man who didn't speak in the meeting.

---

## dhajjar (n-dhajjar)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of dahar
    **MT**: Dahar żgħir u ċkejken fil-forma.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dhajjar kien iġorr borża żgħira minn wara.
    EN: The small back carried a small bag behind.

## dhin (n-dhin)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-h-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of dehen: anointment
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' dehen, l-att u r-rit ta' dilka b'żejt imberrek (anointment).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dhin tas-sider bil-fwieħa sar fl-isptar.
    EN: The anointment of the chest with perfume took place in hospital.

## dhur (n-dhur)
- **POS**: noun
- **Root**: d-h-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of deher
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' deher, it-tnaqqis u l-ħruġ fid-dawl.
  - **EN**: plural of dahar
    **MT**: Plurali tal-kelma dahar.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dhur tal-monument fil-pjazza ġab ferħ kbir.

## dhura (n-dhura)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: an apparition
    **MT**: Dehra jew viżjoni mhux mistennija u sagra.
  - **EN**: plural of dahar
    **MT**: Dehra jew viżjoni mhux mistennija u sagra.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm dhura storika fl-għalqa fejn inbniet il-kappella.
    EN: There was a historic apparition in the field where the chapel was built.

## dib (n-dib)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: wolf
    **MT**: Annimal selvaġġ predatorju bil-fer u bis-snien mill-familja Canidae (wolf).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dib kien jgħajjat mal-lejl fil-muntanji.
    EN: The wolf was howling at night in the mountains.

## dibattibbli (adj-dibattibbli)

- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debatable
    **MT**: Suġġett jew deċiżjoni li tista' tiġi diskussa u miftuħa għal dibattitu (debatable).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Point dibattibbli fil-proposta tal-kunsill.

## dibattiment (n-dibattiment)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debate
    **MT**: L-att u l-proċess ta' dibattitu pubbliku u diskussjoni, dibattitu.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dibattiment parlamentari dam sa tard matul il-lejl.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: debate
    **MT**: Diskussjoni uffiċjali u skambju ta' argumenti f'konferenza jew fil-qorti (debate).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dibattitu kien organizzat bejn żewġ kandidati.
    EN: The debate was organized between two candidates.

## dibsi (adj-dibsi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-b-s
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: colourless, opaque
    **MT**: Kulur kannella jew griż skur simili għall-kulur tad-dibs jew tal-għasel imsajjar (tawny / bay).
  - **EN**: mousy
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' mousy.
  - **EN**: tawny
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' tawny.
  - **EN**: muted, dark, brownish grey colour
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' muted, dark, brownish grey colour.
  - **EN**: bay (of reddish-brown colour)
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' bay.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien żiemel ta' kulur dibsi bil-fer lixx.
    EN: It was a bay-coloured horse with smooth fur.

## dieb (v-dieb)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to melt (become liquid, especially through warmth)
    **MT**: Dab u sar likwidu taħt is-sħana (forma alternattiva ta' dab).
  - **EN**: to disappear
    **MT**: Dab u sar likwidu taħt is-sħana (forma alternattiva ta' dab).
  - **EN**: to become emaciated
    **MT**: Dab u sar likwidu taħt is-sħana (forma alternattiva ta' dab).
  - **EN**: to show tender feelings
    **MT**: Dab u sar likwidu taħt is-sħana (forma alternattiva ta' dab).
- **Alternative Forms**: dab
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Is-silġ dieb fuq il-blat wara s-sebħ.
    EN: The ice melted on the rocks after sunrise.

## diefa (n-diefa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-f-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: mild weather (especially in wintertime)
    **MT**: Temperatura u klima sħuna u pjaċevoli fix-xitwa (mild weather).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Gawdew id-diefa tax-xemx fil-ġnien matul ix-xitwa.
    EN: They enjoyed the mild weather of the sun in the garden during winter.

## diefa (v-diefa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-f-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become warm or tepid
    **MT**: Sirt sħun, biered jew pjaċevoli fit-temperatura (warm up).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-ilma diefa wara ftit minuti fuq in-nar.
    EN: The water warmed up after a few minutes on the fire.

---

## diefi (adj-diefi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-f-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: pleasantly warm, but not too hot
    **MT**: Pjaċevolment sħun mingħajr ma jkun jaħraq wisq (pleasantly warm).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienet ġurnata diefija fl-aħħar ta' Frar.
    EN: It was a pleasantly warm day at the end of February.

---

## dieheb (v-dieheb)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-h-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to gild
    **MT**: Għamel deheb u lita wiċċ b meżizzjoni fuq il-metall.
  - **EN**: to coat with or turn into gold
    **MT**: Għamel deheb u lita wiċċ b meżizzjoni fuq il-metall.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dieheb il-gwarniċ tar-ritratt b'kurakbira.
    EN: He gilded the picture frame with great care.

---

## dieher (adj-dieher)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-h-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: evident, obvious
    **MT**: Li jidher ċar f'għajnejn kulħadd u bla ebda dubju (evident / obvious).
  - **EN**: apparent, visible
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' apparent, visible.
  - **EN**: overt
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' overt.
  - **EN**: transparent
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' transparent.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Fattur dieher li kulħadd innota mill-ewwel.
    EN: An evident factor that everyone noticed right away.

## dieher (v-dieher)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-h-r
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to make something to appear
    **MT**: Għamilt azzjoni biex oġġett isir viżibbli f'għajnejn l-oħrajn.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dieher il-mudell il-ġdid lill-klijenti minnufih.
    EN: He made the new model visible to the clients right away.

---

## diehex (v-diehex)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-h-x
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to startle
    **MT**: Stordajt u tajt sorpriża kbira lil persuna, dehex.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Diehex lin-nies kollha fis-sala bil-ħoss.
    EN: He startled everyone in the hall with the sound.

---

## dielja (n-dielja)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: vine
    **MT**: Siġra jew arbust li jikber u jitla' bil-friegħi u li jagħti l-għeneb (vine).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dielja kienet mimlija għeneb ħelu fil-ħarifa.
    EN: The vine was full of sweet grapes in autumn.

---

## dieq (v-dieq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to taste (perceive the taste of)
    **MT**: Daq u għaraf it-togħma tal-ikel (forma alternattiva ta' daq).
  - **EN**: to taste, try (eat or drink a bit of in order to check the taste)
    **MT**: Daq u għaraf it-togħma tal-ikel (forma alternattiva ta' daq).
  - **EN**: to experience, to go through
    **MT**: Daq u għaraf it-togħma tal-ikel (forma alternattiva ta' daq).
- **Alternative Forms**: daq
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dieq il-frott il-ġdid u sabu ħelu ħafna.
    EN: He tasted the new fruit and found it very sweet.

## dierek (v-dierek)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-r-k
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to get up early
    **MT**: Qomt kmieni fid-dawl tal-ġurnata biex tibda x-xogħol.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dierek kmieni u mar fir-raba' mar-raġġi tax-xemx.
    EN: He got up early and went to the field with the sun rays.

---

## dieċi (n-dieċi)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: ten (used only in games)
    **MT**: Għadd u valur ta' għaxra użat f'ċerti logħob tradizzjonali tal-karti.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kiseb id-dieċi fil-logħba u rebaħ il-punt.
    EN: He got the ten in the game and won the point.

## difa (n-difa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-f-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: mild weather (especially in wintertime)
    **MT**: Sħana u klima pjaċevoli fix-xitwa, diefa.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-difa tax-xemx ġabet kumdità fil-kamra.
    EN: The warmth of the sun brought comfort to the room.

## difa (v-difa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-f-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become warm or tepid
    **MT**: Sirt sħun fit-temperatura, diefa.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-ilma difa matul il-jum fil-faċilità.
    EN: The water warmed up during the day in the facility.

## difen (v-difen)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-f-n
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to bury
    **MT**: Poġġejt u ksejt ġisem mejjet fil-qabar ta taħt l-art (bury).
  - **EN**: to hide [figurative]
    **MT**: Ħbejt jew daħħalt oġġett f'post segret maħżun.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Difnu l-monita l-antika taħt il-ġebla fil-ġnien.
    EN: They buried the ancient coin under the stone in the garden.

## difer (n-difer)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: nail
    **MT**: Parti iebsa u protettiva magħmula mill-keratina fit-tarf tas-swaba' tal-id u tas-sieq (nail).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Qata' difru b'sengħa qabel ma ħasel idejh.
    EN: He cut his nail skilfully before washing his hands.

## differentement (adv-differentement)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: differently
    **MT**: B'mod differenti u f'direzzjoni jew mod ieħor (differently).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Aġixxa differentement minn kif kien mistenni minnu.
    EN: He acted differently from how was expected of him.

## differenti (adj-differenti)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: different
    **MT**: Li mhux l-istess bħal ieħor u li għandu karatteristiċi li jagħżluh (different).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm żewġ ideat differenti fil-laqgħa uffiċjali.
    EN: There were two different ideas in the official meeting.

## differentjali (adj-differentjali)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: differential
    **MT**: Li ikun jappartjeni għad-differenza jew fil-matematika fil-kalkolu (differential).
- **Alternative Forms**: divrenzjali
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kalkolu differentjali użat fl-inġinerija u fis-sistemi.

## differenza (n-differenza)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: difference
    **MT**: In-nuqqas ta' xebh jew l-ammont mibdul bejn żewġ affarijiet jew numri (difference).
- **Alternative Forms**: divrenzja
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kien hemm differenza kbira fil-prezzijiet tal-prodotti.
    EN: There was a big difference in the product prices.

## diffikultuż (adj-diffikultuż)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: difficult
    **MT**: Kompitu jew esperjenza mimlija diffikultajiet u xogħol iebes (difficult).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Proċess diffikultuż li kien jeħtieġ sforz intensiv.
    EN: A difficult process that required intensive effort.

## diffikultà (n-diffikultà)

## diffiċilment (adv-diffiċilment)

## diffiċli (adj-diffiċli)
- **POS**: adjective

  - **EN**: hard
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' hard.
- **Usage Examples**:

## diffużjoni (n-diffużjoni)
- **POS**: noun

## difiża (n-difiża)
