## Diċembru (n-diċembru)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: December
    **MT**: It-tnaqqis u l-aħħar xahar tas-sena fil-kalendarju b'31 jum (December).
- **Alternative Forms**: Ċimber
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Fi Diċembru niċċelebraw il-festa tal-Milied fis-serata.
    EN: In December we celebrate the Christmas feast in the evening.

---

## diġeribbli (adj-diġeribbli)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: digestible
    **MT**: Ikel li mil-faċli li jiġi ddiġerit fl-istonku u fil-ġisem (digestible).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ikel diġeribbli ħafna adattat għall-pazjenti fl-isptar.
    EN: Very digestible food suitable for hospital patients.

---

## diġestiv (adj-diġestiv)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: digestive
    **MT**: Li jappartjeni għas-sistema tad-diġestjoni fl-organiżmu (digestive).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Is-sistema diġestiva teħtieġ ilma u nutrijenti b'saħħithom.
    EN: The digestive system requires water and healthy nutrients.

---

## diġestjoni (n-diġestjoni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: digestion
    **MT**: Il-proċess ta' tkissir u assorbiment tal-ikel fl-istonku u fil-muskoli.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-diġestjoni saret b'mod normali wara l-ikla.
    EN: Digestion proceeded normally after the meal.

---

## diġà (adv-diġà)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: already
    **MT**: Sa dan il-ħin minn qabel u qabel il-mument kurrenti (already).
- **Alternative Forms**: ġiġà
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Diġà tlestejna bil-proġett nazzjonali tagħna.
    EN: We have already finished with our national project.

---

## diżastru (n-diżastru)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: disaster, fiasco
    **MT**: Inċident jew mument ta' ħsara u telf kbir ħafna minn maltempata jew gwerra (disaster).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-maltempata kienet diżastru sħiħ għall-agrikoltura.
    EN: The storm was a complete disaster for agriculture.

---

## diżattent (adj-diżattent)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: inattentive
    **MT**: Persuna li tinsab maħsuba f'oġġett ieħor u nieqsa mill-attenzjoni, distratt.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Aġir diżattent fuq ix-xogħol li jista' jikkawża inċident.
    EN: Inattentive behaviour at work that could cause an accident.

---

## diżgrazzja (n-diżgrazzja)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: tragedy, severe accident
    **MT**: Inċident serju u sfortunat li jġib miegħu uġigħ u telf f'familja (tragedy / accident).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għaddew minn diżgrazzja kbira meta tilfu d-dar tagħhom.
    EN: They went through a great tragedy when they lost their house.

---

## diżunur (n-diżunur)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: disgrace, shame
    **MT**: Telf ta' rispett u reputazzjoni morali minħabba mġiba mhux xierqa (disgrace / shame).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dak l-attitudni ġab diżunur fuq il-familja kollha.
    EN: That attitude brought disgrace upon the whole family.

---

## djaknu (n-djaknu)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deacon
    **MT**: Uffiċjal ordnat fil-knisja li jassisti lill-qassis fil-ministeru reliġjuż (deacon).
- **Alternative Forms**: djakunu
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-djaknu saħħaq iċ-ċerimonja matul il-quddiesa.
    EN: The deacon assisted the ceremony during mass.

---

## djakonessa (n-djakonessa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: female equivalent of djaknu: deaconess
    **MT**: Mara u uffiċjal fil-ministeru reliġjuż tal-knisja (deaconess).
- **Alternative Forms**: djakunessa
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-djakonessa għenet fil-proġetti ta' karità mar-raħal.
    EN: The deaconess helped in charity projects in the village.

---

## djakonija (n-djakonija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deaconship, ministry
    **MT**: Il-ministeru u s-servizz ta' karità u għajnuna offrut fil-parroċċa (deaconship).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-djakonija tal-parroċċa qassmet l-ikel lin-nies meħtieġa.
    EN: The parish deaconship distributed food to needy people.

---

## djakunat (n-djakunat)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diaconate
    **MT**: L-uffiċċju u l-grad reliġjuż tad-djaknu (diaconate).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Irċieva d-djakunat wara li temm l-istudji teoloġiċi.
    EN: He received the diaconate after completing theological studies.

---

## djakunessa (n-djakunessa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: female equivalent of djaknu: deaconess
    **MT**: Uffiċjal mara fil-knisja, djakonessa.
- **Alternative Forms**: djakonessa
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-djakunessa rċeviet tifħir għas-servizz tagħha.
    EN: The deaconess received praise for her service.

---

## djakunu (n-djakunu)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: deacon
    **MT**: Uffiċjal fil-knisja, djaknu.
- **Alternative Forms**: djaknu
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-djakunu ppreżenta l-qari tal-Evanġelju.
    EN: The deacon presented the Gospel reading.

---

## djalett (n-djalett)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dialect
    **MT**: Forma u varjetà uffiċjali ta' lingwa mitkellma f'reġjun jew raħal partikolari (dialect).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-djalett Għawdxi għandu vokalizzazzjoni speċjali u unika.
    EN: The Gozitan dialect has a special and unique vocalization.

---

## djamant (n-djamant)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diamond
    **MT**: Ġawhra u kristall tal-karbonju ta' valur immensament kbir li jilma (diamond).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Iċ-ċirku kien imżejjen b'djamant pur f'nofsu.
    EN: The ring was decorated with a pure diamond in its centre.

---

## djamantin (adj-djamantin)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diamond
    **MT**: Li għandu l-ebusija, d-dija jew il-kwalità tad-djamant (diamond-like).
  - **EN**: diamond - like
    **MT**: Li għandu proprjetà jew kwalità ta' diamond - like.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienet dija djamantina li sorpriżet l-għajnejn.
    EN: It was a diamond-like shine that surprised the eyes.

---

## djanjosi (n-djanjosi)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diagnosis
    **MT**: Identifikazzjoni medika ta' marda, dijanjosi.
- **Alternative Forms**: dijanjosi
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-djanjosi saret fit-tieni jum fl-isptar.
    EN: The diagnosis was made on the second day in hospital.

---

## djanjostiku (adj-djanjostiku)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diagnostic
    **MT**: Li jintuża biex jiddijanjostika marda, dijanjostiku.
- **Alternative Forms**: dijanjostiku
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Tagħmir djanjostiku modern fl-isptar ġenerali.
    EN: Modern diagnostic equipment in the general hospital.

---

## djieq (v-djieq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-j-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to become narrow
    **MT**: Sirt narrow u naqset il-wisa' ta' spazju jew triq minn taħt, dejjaq.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-passaġġ djieq hekk kif daħal bejn il-bini.
    EN: The passage narrowed as it entered between the buildings.

---

## djuq (n-djuq)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-j-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: narrowness
    **MT**: Nuqqas ta' wisa' u l-kwalità ta' spazju li huwa narrow ħafna (narrowness).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-djuq tat-triq ma ppermettiex il-passaġġ ta' trakkijiet.
    EN: The narrowness of the street did not allow the passage of trucks.

---

## djuqija (n-djuqija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-j-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: narrowness
    **MT**: Nuqqas ta' wisa' kif ukoll attitudni riġida f'ideat.
  - **EN**: prudishness
    **MT**: Nuqqas ta' wisa' kif ukoll attitudni riġida f'ideat.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-djuqija tat-triq kinet tagħmilha diffiċli għall-karozzi.
    EN: The narrowness of the street was making it difficult for cars.

---

## dkejkna (n-dkejkna)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: diminutive of dikkiena or dokkiena
    **MT**: Bank tal-ġebel żgħir u ċkejken faċċata ta' dar, dikkiena.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kienet dkejkna żgħira quddiem il-faċċata tradizzjonali.
    EN: It was a tiny stone bench in front of the traditional façade.

---

## dlam (n-dlam)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-l-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: darkness
    **MT**: Nuqqas ta' dawl fil-lejl jew f'kamra bla twieqi.
  - **EN**: obscurity, ignorance [figurative]
    **MT**: Nuqqas ta' dawl fil-lejl jew f'kamra bla twieqi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dlam tal-lejl beda jinfirex bil-mod fuq ir-raħal.
    EN: The darkness of the night started spreading slowly over the village.

---

## dliel (n-dliel)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-l-l
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: long hair [archaic]
    **MT**: Xagħar twil u dens fuq l-ispallejn tal-bniedem jew mar-ras ta' iljun (long hair).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kellha dliel twil u f'kulur kannella mill-isbaħ.
    EN: She had long, beautiful brown hair.

---

## dlik (n-dlik)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-l-k
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: verbal noun of dilek: smearing ointment
    **MT**: In-nom verbal ta' dilek, il-proċess ta' kisja minn krema fuq il-ġilda (smearing).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dlik bil-krema rrazzjonat kumdità kbira.
    EN: The smearing with cream brought great comfort.

---

## dlonk (adv-dlonk)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: at once, directly, immediately
    **MT**: F'dak il-mument u minnufih mingħajr ebda stennija (at once / immediately).
  - **EN**: often, frequently
    **MT**: B'mod li għandu x'jaqsam ma' often, frequently.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Seħħ dlonk u bagħat ir-risposta fl-posta.
    EN: He acted at once and sent the response in the post.

---

## dlumi (adj-dlumi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-l-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dark, obscure
    **MT**: Li ikun fid-dlam jew nieqes mill-dawl u bl-ebda viżibilità, dalmi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kamra dlumija fejn kien diffiċli biex taqra il-kotba.
    EN: A dark room where it was difficult to read books.

---

## dment (conj-dment)
- **POS**: conjunction
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: whilst, as long as
    **MT**: Konġunzjoni li tfisser 'matul il-ħin li' jew 'mentri' (whilst / as long as).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Aġixxa b'dixxiplina dment li kien qed jaħdem fit-tim.
    EN: He acted with discipline whilst he was working in the team.

---

## dmewwi (adj-dmewwi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-m-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sanguinary
    **MT**: Li jappartjeni għad-demm jew bir-riżultati ta' tixrid ta' demm, demmi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Battalja dmewwija li stabbiliet telfiet kbar fl-armata.
    EN: A sanguinary battle that established heavy losses in the army.

---

## dmir (n-dmir)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: duty
    **MT**: L-obbligu, ir-responsabbiltà u d-dover uffiċjali jew morali li dwaru bniedem irid jaġixxi (duty).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Attenda għad-dmir tiegħu fl-uffiċċju b'lealtà kbira.
    EN: He attended to his duty in the office with great loyalty.

---

## dmumi (adj-dmumi)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Root**: d-m-m
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sanguinary
    **MT**: Li għandu r-relazzjoni mad-demm, dmewwi.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Inċident dmumi li stabbilixxa periklu fil-baħar.
    EN: A sanguinary incident that established danger at sea.

---

## dnewwa (n-dnewwa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Root**: d-n-w
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: force used against someone
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' forza jew użu ta' qawwa kontra xi ħadd (forza).
  - **EN**: violence
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' forza jew użu ta' qawwa kontra xi ħadd (forza).
  - **EN**: sanction
    **MT**: Azzjoni ta' forza jew użu ta' qawwa kontra xi ħadd (forza).
- **Alternative Forms**: dnija
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Użaw dnewwa u forza biex jidħlu fil-post uffiċjali.
    EN: They used force to enter the official location.

---

## dnija (n-dnija)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: force used against someone
    **MT**: Użu ta' forza u violenza (forma alternattiva ta' dnewwa).
  - **EN**: violence
    **MT**: Użu ta' forza u violenza (forma alternattiva ta' dnewwa).
  - **EN**: sanction
    **MT**: Użu ta' forza u violenza (forma alternattiva ta' dnewwa).
- **Alternative Forms**: dnewwa
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dnija kienet ikkundannata mil-liġi u mill-poplu.
    EN: The violence was condemned by law and by the people.

---

## dnub (n-dnub)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: d-n-b
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: sin
    **MT**: Ksur tal-liġi reliġjuża jew morali ta' Alla u s-soċjetà.
  - **EN**: moral guilt
    **MT**: Ksur tal-liġi reliġjuża jew morali ta' Alla u s-soċjetà.
  - **EN**: plural of denb (“ tail ”)
    **MT**: Ksur tal-liġi reliġjuża jew morali ta' Alla u s-soċjetà.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-dnub kien meqjus bħala att li jeħtieġ maħfra u ritaljazzjoni.
    EN: Sin was considered an act requiring forgiveness and restitution.

---

## Dodoma (n-dodoma)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Dodoma (the capital city of Tanzania)
    **MT**: Il-belt kapitali uffiċjali tat-Tanżanija fl-Afrika tal-Lvant (Dodoma).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dodoma hija l-kapitali uffiċjali tat-Tanżanija.
    EN: Dodoma is the official capital of Tanzania.

## dokkiena (n-dokkiena)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bench made of stone or wood
    **MT**: Bank tal-ġebel ma' faċċata ta' dar, dikkiena.
- **Alternative Forms**: dikkiena
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Joqogħdu fuq id-dokkiena fit-tmiem tal-ġurnata.
    EN: They sit on the stone bench at the end of the day.

## dolliegħ (n-dolliegħ)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: watermelon (as a mass or species)
    **MT**: Frott kbir tal-ħarifa bil-qoxra ħadra u l-laħam aħmar u ħelu (sinonimu ta' dulliegħ).
  - **EN**: several watermelons
    **MT**: Frott kbir tal-ħarifa bil-qoxra ħadra u l-laħam aħmar u ħelu (sinonimu ta' dulliegħ).
- **Alternative Forms**: dulliegħ
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Kilt dolliegħ frisk f'nofs il-ġurnata tas-sajf.
    EN: I ate fresh watermelon in the middle of a summer day.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: feminine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: damson
    **MT**: Frott tat-tip pruna bil-kulur vjola skur, damaskina.
- **Alternative Forms**: damaskina
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ġabru domaskina mis-siġra tar-razzett.
    EN: They collected damsons from the farm tree.

## domestikabbli (adj-domestikabbli)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: domesticable
    **MT**: Annimal selvaġġ li jista' jiġi ddomestikat u mrobbi mid-dar (domesticable).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Speċi domestikabbli li rriżultat li tgħix mal-bnedmin.
    EN: A domesticable species that turned out to live with humans.

## domestikament (adv-domestikament)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: domestically
    **MT**: B'mod li jappartjeni għad-dar, ir-razzett jew ir-reġjun nazzjonali (domestically).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Prodott prodott domestikament fil-gżejjer.
    EN: A product produced domestically on the islands.

## domestiku (adj-domestiku)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: domestic
    **MT**: Li ikun relatat mad-dar, mal-familja jew mas-suq nazzjonali (domestic).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Annimal domestiku li jgħix mal-familja fid-dar.
    EN: A domestic animal that lives with the family in the house.

## donn (adv-donn)
- **POS**: adverb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: seemingly, by appearance
    **MT**: Adverbju użat fil-Malti li tfisser 'jidher li' jew 'bħallikieku' (seemingly).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Donnu d-dokument se jasal fil-ħin mistenni.
    EN: Seemingly the document will arrive at the expected time.

---

## doq (v-doq)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: d-w-q
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to taste (perceive the taste of)
    **MT**: Daq u għaraf it-togħma tal-ikel (forma alternattiva ta' daq).
  - **EN**: to taste, try (eat or drink a bit of in order to check the taste)
    **MT**: Daq u għaraf it-togħma tal-ikel (forma alternattiva ta' daq).
  - **EN**: to experience, to go through
    **MT**: Daq u għaraf it-togħma tal-ikel (forma alternattiva ta' daq).
- **Alternative Forms**: daq
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Doq l-inbid il-ġdid u qal li kien eċċellenti.
    EN: He tasted the new wine and said it was excellent.

---

## doqqajs (n-doqqajs)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: bee brood
    **MT**: Post jew kaxxa tal-injam fejn ir-rabbi n-naħal biex jagħmlu l-għasel.
  - **EN**: beehive
    **MT**: Post jew kaxxa tal-injam fejn ir-rabbi n-naħal biex jagħmlu l-għasel.
- **Alternative Forms**: duqqajs
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ir-rabbi n-naħal iddilwa d-doqqajs fil-ġnien prinċipali.

## doqqajsa (n-doqqajsa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: queen bee
    **MT**: In-naħla prinċipali u r-reġina fil-kaxxa tad-doqqajs (queen bee).
- **Alternative Forms**: duqqajsa
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Id-doqqajsa kienet imdawra min-naħal kollu.
    EN: The queen bee was surrounded by all the bees.

## dorbi (n-dorbi)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: lizard [dialectal]
    **MT**: Rettili żgħir li jimxi fuq il-ħitan, dirbi / lizard.
- **Alternative Forms**: dirbi
- **Usage Examples**:

    EN: The lizard was on the rock catching insects.

## Dorbies (n-dorbies)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: Leo (constellation)

  - **EN**: Leo (zodiac sign)
    **MT**: Is-sinjal u l-kostellazzjoni ta' Leo fl-astronomija (ortografija alternattiva ta' Dirbies).

    EN: Leo is one of the zodiac signs in astronomy.

- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: dorsal
    **MT**: Li jappartjeni għad-dahar fl-anatomija u l-bijoloġija (dorsal).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Pinen dorsali fuq dahar il-delfin fil-baħar.
    EN: Dorsal fin on the dolphin's back in the sea.

## Dott. (n-dott)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: abbreviation of duttur: Dr.
    **MT**: Abrevjazzjoni uffiċjali u titlu użat għat-tabib jew tabiba (Dr.).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Dott. Borg flimkien max-xogħol mediku fl-isptar.
    EN: Dr Borg along with medical work in hospital.

## dottorali (adj-dottorali)
- **POS**: adjective
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: doctoral
    **MT**: Li ikun relatat mal-grad għoli ta' dottorat fl-università (doctoral).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Teżi dottorali li rċeviet tifħir kbir mill-professuri.
    EN: A doctoral thesis that received great praise from professors.

## dottorat (n-dottorat)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: doctorate
    **MT**: L-ogħla grad akkademiku mogħti mill-università wara riċerka (doctorate).
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Obta d-dottorat tiegħu fil-kimika fl-università.
    EN: He obtained his doctorate in chemistry at university.

## dottur (n-dottur)

## dqiq (n-dqiq)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine

## dqiqa (n-dqiqa)
- **POS**: noun

- **Gender**: masculine

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Fil-ħrafa, id-dragun kien jgħix fil-quċċata tal-muntanja.

- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Rranġaw id-dranaġġ tat-triq wara l-ħsara.

- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine

  - **EN**: habit

  - **EN**: to be hung (intransitive) to be killed by hanging
    **MT**: Sospiż minn rinfors u mfassal bil-forma ta' dendla (mediopassiva).

- **Alternative Forms**: ddendel
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Iddendel il-kwadru mat-tarf tal-ħajt bil-ħabel.
